&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|P_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}+3)}}\left\langle l,m\right|Z_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&-\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|Z_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|M_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\\
&\left.+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|M_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\right]\\
&\times\left[\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left[\delta_{0,s}\tau^{I}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s}\right)\chi^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.1.2c)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{1,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}\left[\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right.\\
&+\left.\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(r)}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\right]-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(1)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}R_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\tilde{Y}_{(1)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}\left[\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(r)}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\right]\\
&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left[\delta_{0,s}\tau^{I}\right.\\
&\left.+\left(1-\delta_{0,s}\right)\chi^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\left[\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left\langle l,m\right|R_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.1.2d)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{2,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\frac{\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\left[\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\frac{P_{+}^{2}}{(r-r_{+})^{2}}\right.\\
&\left.+\frac{P_{-}^{2}}{(r-r_{-})^{2}}-\frac{A_{+}}{(r_{+}-r_{-})(r-r_{+})}+\frac{A_{-}}{(r_{+}-r_{-})(r-r_{-})}\right.\\
&\left.-(\mu_{V}^{2}-\omega^{2})-\frac{l(l+1)}{\Delta(r)}\right]\,.\end{aligned}

(A.1.2e)

The couplings for the tensor case (
S=2
), instead, read:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \Gamma_{l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(2)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\left[\tilde{S}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}+\tilde{Q}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\mathbfcal{L}_{\boldsymbol{z}}+\tilde{R}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}\\

&\times\left[\left\langle l,m\right|\left(\tilde{S}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}+\tilde{R}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right)\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle+m\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{m^{\prime}}_{m}\tilde{Q}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.1.3a)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \Lambda_{l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(2)J}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\frac{a^{2}\cos^{2}\theta(\mu_{T}^{2}-\omega^{2})}{\Delta(r)}\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\

(A.1.3b)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} D_{l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(2)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\left[\tilde{P}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{+}}+\tilde{Z}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{0}}+\tilde{M}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{-}}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}\\
=&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\

&\left.+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{M}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\right]\\
&\times\left[\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},1})\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left[\delta_{s,1}\tau^{I}_{s,s_{z}}+(1-\delta_{s,1})\chi^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.1.3c)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{1,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}\left[\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right.\\

=&\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}\left[\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\frac{\zeta^{\prime\prime}(r)}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\right]\\
&-\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left[\delta_{s,1}\tau^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right.\\
&\left.+(1-\delta_{s,1})\chi^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\left[\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+(1-\delta_{s,1})\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{R}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.1.3d)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{2,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\frac{\delta^{l^{\prime}}_{l}\delta^{s^{\prime}}_{s}\delta^{j^{\prime}}_{j}}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\left[\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\left(\frac{1}{r-r_{+}}+\frac{1}{r-r_{-}}\right)\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\frac{P_{+}^{2}}{(r-r_{+})^{2}}\right.\\
&\left.+\frac{P_{-}^{2}}{(r-r_{-})^{2}}-\frac{A_{+}}{(r_{+}-r_{-})(r-r_{+})}+\frac{A_{-}}{(r_{+}-r_{-})(r-r_{-})}\right.\\
&\left.-(\mu_{T}^{2}-\omega^{2})-\frac{l(l+1)}{\Delta(r)}\right]\,.\end{aligned}

(A.1.3e)

A.2
Couplings in spin-1 and spin-2 Lorenz constraints

Here we show the full expressions of the couplings appearing in
3.4.8a
and
3.4.8b
:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{3,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\rho^{J}\tilde{Y}_{(1)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\rho^{J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1a)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{3,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(1)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\tilde{\rho}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\rho}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\delta_{s,0}{\tau}^{I}+(1-\delta_{s,0}){\chi}^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\left[\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},1})\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1b)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{4,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left[\rho^{J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\sigma^{J}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(1)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left[\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\\
&\times\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\left[\rho^{J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\sigma^{J}\right]\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1c)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{4,l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(1)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left[\tilde{\rho}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\tilde{\sigma}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\delta_{s,0}{\tau}^{I}+(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},0}){\chi}^{J}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\left[\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},1})\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\\
&\times\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left\langle l,m\right|\left[\tilde{\rho}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+\tilde{\sigma}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\right]\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1d)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \tilde{D}_{j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left[\pi^{J}\boldsymbol{D_{+}}+\zeta^{J}\boldsymbol{D_{0}}+\mu^{J}\boldsymbol{D_{-}}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(1)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\pi^{J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle+\right.\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\pi^{J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}+3)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\zeta^{J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&-\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\zeta^{J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\mu^{J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\\
&\left.+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\mu^{J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\right]\\
&\times\left[\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{0,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1e)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \tilde{D}_{l,s,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)&=\int d\Omega\left(Y^{(1)I}_{l,s,j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left[\tilde{\pi}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{+}}+\tilde{\zeta}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{0}}+\tilde{\mu}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{-}}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
&=\sum\limits_{m,s_{z},m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\left\langle l,m,s,s_{z}\right|\left.j,j_{z}\right\rangle\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\pi}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle+\right.\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\pi}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}+3)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\zeta}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&-\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\zeta}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\mu}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\\
&\left.+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle l,m\right|\tilde{\mu}_{I}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\right]\\
&\times\left[\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},1})\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left[\delta_{s,0}{\tau}^{I}+(1-\delta_{s,0}){\chi}^{I}_{s,s_{z}}\right]\,,\end{aligned}

(A.2.1f)

A.3
Couplings of the spin-2 second-order constraint

In this section we show the full formulae of the radial couplings appearing in equations
3.4.9
:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{5,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}R^{J}\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\\
&\times\left[\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|R^{J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.3.1a)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{6,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\left[R^{J}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{\tilde{R}^{J}}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left[\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\\
&\times\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\left[R^{J}\frac{2F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)\zeta^{\prime}(r)}+\frac{\tilde{R}^{J}}{\zeta^{\prime}(r)}\right]\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.3.1b)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} C_{7,j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\left[A^{J}l^{\prime}(l^{\prime}+1)+\tilde{S}^{J}+\tilde{Q}^{J}\mathbfcal{L}_{\boldsymbol{z}}+\tilde{R}^{J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right.\\
&\left.+R^{J}\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\left[\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\tilde{\tau}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}+\left(1-\delta_{s^{\prime},1}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\times\\
&\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\left[l^{\prime}(l^{\prime}+1)A^{J}+\tilde{S}^{J}+m^{\prime}\tilde{Q}^{J}+\tilde{R}^{J}\frac{F^{\prime}(r)}{F(r)}+R^{J}\frac{F^{\prime\prime}(r)}{F(r)}\right]\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.3.1c)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} L_{j}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime}}(r)=&\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\int d\Omega\left(Y_{j,j_{z}}\right)^{*}\left[\tilde{P}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{+}}+\tilde{Z}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{0}}+\tilde{M}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\boldsymbol{D_{-}}\right.\\
&\left.+\tilde{P}^{J}\mathbfcal{L}_{+}+\tilde{M}^{J}\mathbfcal{L}_{-}\right]\tilde{Y}_{(2)J}^{l^{\prime},s^{\prime},j^{\prime},j_{z}}=\\
=&\sum\limits_{m^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\left[\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{P}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle+\right.\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{P}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}+3)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{Z}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&-\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})}{(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{Z}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{(l^{\prime}+1)^{2}(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}-1)}{2(2l^{\prime}+1)(2l^{\prime}-1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{M}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}-1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{l^{\prime 2}(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+2)}{2(2l^{\prime}+3)(2l^{\prime}+1)}}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{M}_{0}^{\penalty\ J}\left|l^{\prime}+1,m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime}+1)}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{P}^{J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}+1\right\rangle\\
&+\sqrt{(l^{\prime}+m^{\prime})(l^{\prime}-m^{\prime}+1)}\left\langle j,j_{z}\right|\tilde{M}^{J}\left|l^{\prime},m^{\prime}-1\right\rangle\Bigg]\\
&\times\left[\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\tilde{\tau}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}_{J}+\left(1-\delta_{1,s^{\prime}}\right)\tilde{\chi}_{J}^{s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}}\right]\frac{1}{\zeta^{\prime 2}(r)}\left\langle l^{\prime},m^{\prime},s^{\prime},s_{z}^{\prime}\right|\left.j^{\prime},j_{z}\right\rangle\,,\end{aligned}

(A.3.1d)

Appendix B
Complements for the bra-ket overlap integrals

B.1
Alternative derivation of the trigonometric spherical harmonic bra-kets

An anonymous referee, to whom we express our gratitude, suggested the following alternative derivation for integrals (
3.5.2a
) and (
3.5.2b
). Legendre polynomials can be expanded as linear combinations of Čebyšëv polynomials via a Fourier transform, whose coefficients can be found in Example 15.1.2 of
[
256
]
:

P_{l}(\cos\theta)=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}a_{l,j}T_{l-2j}(\cos\theta),\quad a_{l,j}=\frac{2(2l-2j-1)!!(2j-1)!!}{(1+\delta_{l-2j,0})(2l-2j)!!(2j)!!}\,.

(B.1.1)

Thus, by exploiting the properties of the product of Čebyšëv polynomials
[
201
]
and using the fact that

\displaystyle\int_{-1}^{1}dxT_{n}(x)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\sin\theta\cos(n\theta)=

(B.1.2)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\frac{\sin((n+1)\theta)-\sin((n-1)\theta)}{2}=

\displaystyle=\frac{(-1)^{n}+1}{1-n^{2}}(1-\delta_{n,1})\,,

we can solve the following integral:

\displaystyle I_{n,l}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\sin\theta\cos(n\theta)P_{l}(\cos\theta)=

(B.1.3)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}a_{l,j}\int_{-1}^{1}dxT_{n}(x)T_{l-2j}(x)=

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}\frac{a_{l,j}}{2}\int_{-1}^{1}dx\left[T_{n+l-2j}(x)+T_{|n-l+2j|}(x)\right]=

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}a_{l,j}\left[\frac{1}{1-(n+l-2j)^{2}}+\frac{1}{1-(n-l+2j)^{2}}\right]\delta_{((n+l)\bmod 2),0}.

Because of the orthogonality of Legendre polynomials
[
201
]
,

\int_{-1}^{1}dxP_{l}(x)P_{l^{\prime}}(x)=\frac{2}{2l+1}\delta_{l,l^{\prime}}\,,

(B.1.4)

the coefficients
b^{n}_{l}
and
a^{n}_{l}
appearing in Eq. (
3.5.6
) can be written in terms of the integrals (
B.1.3
) as follows

b^{n}_{l}=\left(l+\frac{1}{2}\right)I_{n,l}\penalty\ ,\penalty\ \qquad a^{n}_{l}=-\frac{1}{n}\left(l+\frac{1}{2}\right)I_{n,l}\,.

(B.1.5)

Therefore, we get the following alternative expressions for the bra-ket integrals:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\langle l_{1},m_{1}\right|e^{ik\phi}\cos(n\theta)\left|l_{2},m_{2}\right\rangle=\\
&=\sum\limits_{l=|l_{1}-l_{2}|}^{l_{1}+l_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}\frac{(-1)^{m_{1}}}{2}\left(n-2j+\frac{1}{2}\right)I_{n,n-2j}\sqrt{\frac{(2l_{1}+1)(2l_{2}+1)(l+k)!}{(l-k)!}}\\
&\times\left\langle l_{1},-m_{1},l_{2},m_{2}\right|\left.l,-k\right\rangle\left\langle l_{1},0,l_{2},0\right|\left.l,0\right\rangle I(n-2j,0,l,-k)\,,\end{aligned}

(B.1.6a)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\langle l_{1},m_{1}\right|e^{ik\phi}\sin(n\theta)\left|l_{2},m_{2}\right\rangle=\\
&=\sum\limits_{l=|l_{1}-l_{2}|}^{l_{1}+l_{2}}\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}\frac{(-1)^{m_{1}+1}}{2n}\left(n-2j+\frac{1}{2}\right)I_{n,n-2j}\sqrt{\frac{(2l_{1}+1)(2l_{2}+1)(l+k)!}{(l-k)!}}\\
&\times\left\langle l_{1},-m_{1},l_{2},m_{2}\right|\left.l,-k\right\rangle\left\langle l_{1},0,l_{2},0\right|\left.l,0\right\rangle I(n-2j,1,l,-k)\,.\end{aligned}

(B.1.6b)

This alternative derivation is more straightforward than the one we found, but it gives less compact expressions involving double factorials and an additional sum instead of the gamma functions appearing in our solutions. With these alternative formulas we observe a slightly higher computation time with respect to the expressions we found, which can become relevant when there are many integrals of the type (
3.5.2a
) and (
3.5.2b
) to be computed.

B.2
Simplified solutions for the axisymmetric trigonometric spherical harmonic bra-kets

The formulas appearing in the alternative derivation described in
B.1
can be used also for finding simplified expressions in the axisymmetric case (
k=0
). In this simplified case, the key integrals appearing in Eq. (
3.5.5a
) involve just Legendre polynomials instead of Legendre associated functions, thus coinciding with integrals
B.1.3
. Consequently, Eq. (
3.5.5a
) for
k=0
can be reduced to

\displaystyle\left\langle l_{1},m_{1}\right|\cos(n\theta)\left|l_{2},m_{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{l=|l_{1}-l_{2}|}^{l_{1}+l_{2}}\sqrt{\left(l_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(l_{2}+\frac{1}{2}\right)}(-1)^{m_{1}}

(B.2.1)

\displaystyle\times\left\langle l_{1},0,l_{2},0\right|\left.l,0\right\rangle\left\langle l_{1},-m_{1},l_{2},m_{2}\right|\left.l,0\right\rangle I_{n,l}\,.

We can find a similar procedure also for the sine case, shown in Eq. (
3.5.5b
). From the trigonometric product-to-sum formulas we have

\displaystyle\sin\theta\sin(n\theta)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[\cos((n-1)\theta)-\cos((n+1)\theta)\right]=

(B.2.2)

\displaystyle=\frac{T_{n-1}(\cos\theta)-T_{n+1}(\cos\theta)}{2}

and, therefore, the key integrals appearing in (
3.5.5b
) become

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\sin\theta\sin(n\theta)P_{l}(\cos\theta)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}\frac{a_{l,j}}{2}\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\left[T_{n-1}(\cos\theta)-T_{n+1}(\cos\theta)\right]T_{l-2j}(x)=

(B.2.3)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{j=0}^{\lfloor l/2\rfloor}\frac{a_{l,j}}{4}\int_{0}^{\pi}d\theta\Bigl[T_{n-1+l-2j}(\cos\theta)-T_{|n-1-l+2j|}(\cos\theta)

\displaystyle-T_{n+1+l-2j}(\cos\theta)+T_{|n+1-l+2j|}(\cos\theta)\Bigr]=0\,.

Hence, equation (
3.5.5b
) in this case reduces to

\left\langle l_{1},m_{1}\right|\sin(n\theta)\left|l_{2},m_{2}\right\rangle=0\,,

(B.2.4)

which can also be deduced by considering the parity of the functions involved.

B.3
Formulas for the spherical harmonic decomposition of the Regge-Wheeler tensor basis

From
3.2.6
,
4.6.12
,
3.3.6
,
3.3.7a
and
3.3.7b
we get the following expressions for the angular functions appearing in
4.6.8
:

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} X_{j,j_{z}}=&i\left[(j_{z}+1)\sqrt{(j-j_{z})(j+j_{z}+1)}e^{-i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}+1}\right.\\
&\left.-(j_{z}-1)\sqrt{(j+j_{z})(j-j_{z}+1)}e^{i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}-1}\right]\end{aligned}

(B.3.1a)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} W_{j,j_{z}}=&\frac{1}{2}\left\{\sqrt{(j-j_{z})[j^{2}-(j_{z}+1)^{2}](j+j_{z}+2)}e^{-2i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}+2}+\right.\\
&\left.+2j_{z}^{2}Y_{j,j_{z}}+\sqrt{(j+j_{z})[j^{2}-(j_{z}-1)^{2}](j-j_{z}+2)}e^{2i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}-2}\right\}\end{aligned}

(B.3.1b)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \csc\theta\partial_{\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}}=&-\frac{i}{2}\left\{e^{-i\phi}\left[\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j-j_{z})(j-j_{z}-1)}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}Y_{j-1,j_{z}+1}\right.\right.\\
&\left.+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j+j_{z}+1)(j+j_{z}+2)}{(2j+3)(2j+1)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}+1}\right]\\
&+e^{i\phi}\left[\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j+j_{z})(j+j_{z}-1)}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}\right.\\
&\left.\left.\times Y_{j-1,j_{z}-1}+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j-j_{z}+1)(j-j_{z}+2)}{(2j+3)(2j+1)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}-1}\right]\right\}\end{aligned}

(B.3.1c)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \sin\theta\partial_{\theta}Y_{j,j_{z}}=&\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j+j_{z}+1)(j-j_{z}+1)}{(2j+1)(2j+3)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}}\\
&-\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j+j_{z})(j-j_{z})}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}Y_{j-1,j_{z}}\end{aligned}

(B.3.1d)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \partial_{\theta}Y_{j,j_{z}}=\frac{1}{2}\left[\sqrt{(j-j_{z})(j+j_{z}+1)}e^{-i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}+1}-\sqrt{(j+j_{z})(j-j_{z}+1)}e^{i\phi}Y_{j,j_{z}-1}\right]\end{aligned}

(B.3.1e)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \csc\theta X_{j,j_{z}}=&\frac{i}{2}\left\{-\sqrt{(j-j_{z})(j+j_{z}+1)}\left[e^{-2i\phi}\left(\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j-j_{z}-1)(j-j_{z}-2)}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}\right.\right.\right.\\
&\times\left.Y_{j-1,j_{z}+2}+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j+j_{z}+2)(j+j_{z}+3)}{(2j+3)(2j+1)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}+2}\right)\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j-j_{z})(j-j_{z}+1)}{(2j+3)(2j+1)}}\left.Y_{j+1,j_{z}}+\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j+j_{z}+1)(j+j_{z})}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}Y_{j-1,j_{z}}\right]\\
&+\sqrt{(j+j_{z})(j-j_{z}+1)}\Bigg[e^{2i\phi}\left(\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j+j_{z}-1)(j+j_{z}-2)}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}Y_{j-1,j_{z}-2}\right.\\
&+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j-j_{z}+2)(j-j_{z}+3)}{(2j+3)(2j+1)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}-2}\Bigg)+\sqrt{\frac{(j+1)^{2}(j-j_{z}+1)(j-j_{z})}{(2j+1)(2j-1)}}\\
&\times Y_{j-1,j_{z}}+\sqrt{\frac{j^{2}(j+j_{z})(j+j_{z}+1)}{(2j+1)(2j+3)}}Y_{j+1,j_{z}}\Bigg]\Bigg\}\end{aligned}

(B.3.1f)
